Note that, if enqueue\n   107→   *  the failed event at the end of the queue, this event could be processed with quite some delay\n   108→   *  and this is not the behavior we want. lastTimeUpdate > 1000 * PARTITIONS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_IN_SECONDS) {\n   200→      try {\n   201→        badPartitions.clear();\n   202→        badPartitions.add(-1);\n   203→        partitionInfoList = 236→        }\n   237→      }\n   238→      currentPartitionId =  partitionInfoList.get(ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(\n   239→          partitionInfoList.size())).partition();\n   240→      LOG.warn(\"After {} trials, set current partition to {}\",\n   241→          MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION, currentPartitionId);\n   242→\n   243→    }\n   244→  }\n   245→\n   246→  /**\n   247→   *  Sender dequeues LoggingAuditEvents and sends them to Kafka cluster. If send to one partition\n   248→   *  fails, it will choose another partition. For each event, it will try at most\n   249→   *  NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING times (3 times) before dropping this event. Metrics are\n   250→   *  used to track the queue size and usuage, number of events sent out to Kafka successfully, and\n   251→   *  the number of events dropped.\n   252→   */\n   253→  @Override\n   254→  public void run() {\n   255→    LoggingAuditEvent event = null;\n   256→    ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> record;\n   257→    byte[] value = null;\n   258→\n   259→    while (!cancelled.get()) 266→        event = queue.poll(DEQUEUE_WAIT_IN_SECONDS, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n   267→        if (event != null) {\n   268→          try {\n   269→            value = serializer.serialize(event);\n   270→            record = new ProducerRecord<>(this.topic, currentPartitionId , null, value);\n   271→            kafkaProducer.send(record, new KafkaProducerCallback(event, currentPartitionId));\n PARTITIONS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_IN_SECONDS = 30;\n\n  /**\n   *  When sender send audit events to Kafka,  it chooses a random partition and if it fails, it\n   *  will choose another GOOD partition, this retry will happen at most numOfPartitionsToTrySending\n   *  times before dropping the event. Note that, this is different from the retry handled by\n   *  kafka client\n   *  library when sending an event to a certain partition.\n   */\n  private static final int NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING = 3;\n\n  /**\n   *  maximum time to wait when sender tries to dequeue events before returning null.\n   */\n  private static final int DEQUEUE_WAIT_IN_SECONDS = 30;\n\n\n  /**\n   *   when gracefully shutting down the Sender, the calling thread sleeps for some time and let\n   *   the sender to send out audit events left in the queue if there are any.\n   *   stopGracePeriodInSeconds is the maximum time reserved and\n   *   stopGracePeriodInSeconds / THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS is the total rounds the calling thread\n   *   will sleep.\n   */\n  private static final int THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS = 10;\n\n  /**\n   *  when gracefully shutting down the Sender, this field specifies maximum time for main thread\n   *  to wait, in order to let the sender send out audit events left in the queue if there are any.\n   */\n  private int stopGracePeriodInSeconds = 300;\n\n\n  /**\n   * Logging audit stage, can be THRIFTLOGGER, SINGER, MERCED and so on\n   */\n  private final LoggingAuditStage stage;\n\n  /**\n   * host name\n   */\n  private final String host;\n\n  /**\n   *  LinkedBlockingDequeue to store the LoggingAuditEvents. (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastTimeUpdate > 1000 * PARTITIONS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_IN_SECONDS) {\n      try {\n        badPartitions.clear();\n        badPartitions.add(-1);\n        partitionInfoList = this.kafkaProducer.partitionsFor(topic);\n        lastTimeUpdate = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n            LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITIONS_REFRESH_COUNT, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n      } catch (Exception e) {\n Drop this event.\");\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n        LOG.debug(\n            \"Enqueuing LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue was interrupted in callback. \"\n                + \"Drop this event\");\n        eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onCompletion(RecordMetadata recordMetadata, Exception e) {\n      try {\n        if (e == null) {\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_ACKED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n\n          // if send is successful, remove the event from the map eventTriedCount if it was added\n          // LoggingAuditHeaders can uniquely identify an event.\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          // if send out successfully, remove the partition from the badPartitions if it was added.\n          badPartitions.remove(recordMetadata.partition());\n        } else {\n          checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed();\n        }\n      } catch (Throwable t) {\n        LOG.warn(\"Exception throws in the callback. com.pinterest.singer.loggingaudit.thrift.configuration.KafkaSenderConfig;\nimport com.pinterest.singer.metrics.OpenTsdbMetricConverter;\nimport com.pinterest.singer.utils.CommonUtils;\n\nimport java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;\nimport java.util.concurrent.LinkedBlockingDeque;\nimport java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;\nimport java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;\nimport java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;\n\n/**\n *  LoggingAuditEventSender implementations that dequeue LoggingAuditEvent and send it to Kafka.\n *\n *  Each instance of this class should be executed in its own thread.\n */\npublic class AuditEventKafkaSender implements LoggingAuditEventSender {\n\n  private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(AuditEventKafkaSender.class);\n\n  private static final int MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION = 10;\n\n  private static final int PARTITIONS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_IN_SECONDS = 30;\n\n  /**\n   *  When sender send audit events to Kafka,  it chooses a random partition and if it fails, it\n   *  will choose another GOOD partition, this retry will happen at most numOfPartitionsToTrySending\n   *  times before dropping the event. Note that, this is different from the retry handled by\n   *  kafka client\n   *  library when sending an event to a certain partition.\n   */\n  private static final int NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING = 3;\n\n  /**\n   *  maximum time to wait when sender tries to dequeue events before returning null.\n   */\n  private static final int DEQUEUE_WAIT_IN_SECONDS = 30;\n\n\n  /**\n   *   when gracefully shutting down the Sender, the calling thread sleeps for some time and let\n   *   the sender to send out audit events left in the queue if there are any.\n   *   stopGracePeriodInSeconds is the maximum time reserved and\n   *   stopGracePeriodInSeconds / THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS is the total rounds the calling thread\n   *   will sleep.\n   */\n  private static final int THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS = 10;\n\n  /**\n   *  when gracefully shutting down the Sender, this field specifies maximum time for main thread\n   *  to wait, in order to let the sender send out audit events left in the queue if there are any.\n   */\n  private int stopGracePeriodInSeconds = 300;\n\n\n  /**\n   * Logging audit stage, can be THRIFTLOGGER, SINGER, MERCED and so on\n   */\n  private final LoggingAuditStage stage;\n\n  /**\n   * host name\n   */\n  private final String host;\n\n  /**\n   *  LinkedBlockingDequeue to store the LoggingAuditEvents.  The max capacity is specified when\n   *  creating this deque in the LoggingAuditClient.java\n   *\n   *  The AuditEventKafkaSender dequeue from the beginning of the deque, if does not send out event\n   *  successfully, it will enqueue this event to the beginning of the queue.  Note that, if enqueue\n   *  the failed event at the end of the queue, this event could be processed with quite some delay\n   *  and this is not the behavior we want.  That's we use LinkedBlockingDequeue,\n   *  not ArrayBlockingQueue.\n   */\n  private final LinkedBlockingDeque<LoggingAuditEvent> queue;\n\n  /**\n   * KafkaProducer instance to send audit events\n   */\n  private KafkaProducer<byte[], byte[]> kafkaProducer;\n\n  /**\n   * Serialize key and value to byte[]\n   */\n  private TSerializer serializer = new TSerializer();\n\n  /**\n   *  flag to control the start and stop of the executing thread.\n   */\n  private AtomicBoolean cancelled = new AtomicBoolean(false);\n\n  /**\n   *  topic to store the audit events\n   */\n  private String topic;\n\n  /**\n   *  name of this sender instance\n   */\n  private String name;\n\n  /**\n   *  executing thread\n   */\n  private Thread thread;\n\n  /**\n   *  List of PartitionInfo\n   */\n  private List<PartitionInfo> partitionInfoList = new ArrayList<>();\n\n  /**\n   *  last time when partition list was refreshed. we want to refresh partition list every 5 mins.\n   */\n  private long lastTimeUpdate = -1;\n\n  /**\n   *  If sending out to one partition fails, this partition is added to set;\n   *  If sending out to one partition succeeds, this partition is removed if it was added before.\n   */\n  private Set<Integer> badPartitions = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();\n\n  /**\n   * For each event (identified by LoggingAuditHeaders, key of the map), track the number of tries\n   * for sending to Kafka. Each try will choose a different and partition that is not in the\n   * badPartitions.  When event is send out successfully or dropped, the corresponding entry in\n   * this map is removed.\n   */\n\n  private Map<LoggingAuditHeaders, Integer> eventTriedCount = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();\n\n  /**\n   *  currentPartitionId specifies the partition of audit_event topic used to receive audit events.\n   *  The currentPartitionId will be reset in resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded() method. This reduces\n   *  the number of TCP connections from audit client to the Kafka Cluster hosting the audit_event\n   *  topic.\n   */\n  private int currentPartitionId = -1;\n\n  public AuditEventKafkaSender(KafkaSenderConfig config,\n                               LinkedBlockingDeque<LoggingAuditEvent> queue,\n                               LoggingAuditStage stage, String host, String name) {\n    this.topic = config.getTopic();\n    this.queue = queue;\n    this.stage = stage;\n    this.host = host;\n    this.name = name;\n    this.stopGracePeriodInSeconds = config.getStopGracePeriodInSeconds();\n    this.badPartitions.add(-1);\n  }\n\n\n  public KafkaProducer<byte[], byte[]> getKafkaProducer() {\n    return kafkaProducer;\n  }\n\n  public void setKafkaProducer(KafkaProducer<byte[], byte[]> kafkaProducer) {\n    this.kafkaProducer = kafkaProducer;\n  }\n\n  private void refreshPartitionIfNeeded() {\n    // refresh every 30 seconds\n    if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastTimeUpdate > 1000 * PARTITIONS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_IN_SECONDS) {\n      try {\n        badPartitions.clear();\n        badPartitions.add(-1);\n        partitionInfoList = this.kafkaProducer.partitionsFor(topic);\n        lastTimeUpdate = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n            LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITIONS_REFRESH_COUNT, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n      } catch (Exception e) {\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n            LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITIONS_REFRESH_ERROR, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n      }\n    }\n    resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded();\n  }\n\n  private void resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded() {\n    if (partitionInfoList.size() == 0) {\n      currentPartitionId = -1;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (badPartitions.contains(currentPartitionId)){\n      int trial = 0;\n      while (trial < MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION) {\n        trial += 1;\n        int index = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(partitionInfoList.size());\n        int randomPartition = partitionInfoList.get(index).partition();\n        if (!badPartitions.contains(randomPartition)) {\n          LOG.warn(\"Change current partition of audit event topic from {} to {}\", currentPartitionId,\n              randomPartition);\n          currentPartitionId = randomPartition;\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n              LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_CURRENT_PARTITION_RESET, 1,\n              \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n      currentPartitionId =  partitionInfoList.get(ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(\n          partitionInfoList.size())).partition();\n      LOG.warn(\"After {} trials, set current partition to {}\",\n          MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION, currentPartitionId);\n\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  Sender dequeues LoggingAuditEvents and sends them to Kafka cluster. If send to one partition\n   *  fails, it will choose another partition. For each event, it will try at most\n   *  NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING times (3 times) before dropping this event. Metrics are\n   *  used to track the queue size and usuage, number of events sent out to Kafka successfully, and\n   *  the number of events dropped.\n   */\n  @Override\n  public void run() {\n    LoggingAuditEvent event = null;\n    ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> record;\n    byte[] value = null;\n\n    while (!cancelled.get()) {\n      try {\n        refreshPartitionIfNeeded();\n        if (currentPartitionId == -1){\n          Thread.sleep(100);\n          continue;\n        }\n        event = queue.poll(DEQUEUE_WAIT_IN_SECONDS, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n        if (event != null) {\n KafkaProducerCallback(event, currentPartitionId));\n          } catch (TException e) {\n            LOG.debug(\"[{}] failed to construct ProducerRecord because of serialization exception.\",\n                Thread.currentThread().getName(), e);\n            OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n                .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_SERIALIZATION_EXCEPTION, 1,\n                    \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                    \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n            eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException e) {\n        LOG.warn(\"[{}] got interrupted when polling the queue and while loop is ended!\",\n            Thread.currentThread().getName(), e);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n            LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_DEQUEUE_INTERRUPTED_EXCEPTION, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n        break;\n      } catch (Exception e) {\n        LOG.warn(\"Exit the while loop and finish the thread execution due to exception: \", e);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_EXCEPTION, 1,\n            \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n\n  public class KafkaProducerCallback implements Callback {\n\n    private LoggingAuditEvent event;\n    private int partition;\n\n    public KafkaProducerCallback(LoggingAuditEvent event, int partition) {\n      this.event = event;\n      this.partition = partition;\n    }\n\n    public void checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed() {\n      // if exception thrown (i.e. the send failed), the partition is added to badPartitions.\n      badPartitions.add(this.partition);\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n          .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITION_ERROR, 1,\n              \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(), \"topic=\" + topic,\n              \"partition=\" + this.partition);\n\n      // retry the failed event by inserting it at the beginning of the deque.\n      // If number of tries reaches 3, meaning that 3 partitions have been tried sending to but\n      // still failed, this event is dropped.\n      Integer count = eventTriedCount.get(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      if (count == null){\n        eventTriedCount.put(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders(), 1);\n        insertEvent(event);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n            .gauge(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_RETRIED,\n                eventTriedCount.size(), \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                \"topic=\" + topic);\n      } else if (count >= NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING) {\n          LOG.debug(\"Failed to send audit event after trying {} partitions. Drop event.\", count);\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_DROPPED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      } else {\n          eventTriedCount.put(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders(), count + 1);\n          insertEvent(event);\n      }\n    }\n\n    public void insertEvent(LoggingAuditEvent event){\n      try {\n        boolean success = queue.offerFirst(event, 3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n        if (!success) {\n          LOG.debug(\"Failed to enqueue LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue when executing \"\n              + \"producer send callback. Drop this event.\");\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n        LOG.debug(\n            \"Enqueuing LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue was interrupted in callback. \"\n                + \"Drop this event\");\n        eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onCompletion(RecordMetadata recordMetadata, Exception e) {\n      try {\n        if (e == null) {\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_ACKED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n\n          // if send is successful, remove the event from the map eventTriedCount if it was added\n          // LoggingAuditHeaders can uniquely identify an event.\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          // if send out successfully, remove the partition from the badPartitions if it was added.\n          badPartitions.remove(recordMetadata.partition());\n        } else {\n          checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed();\n        }\n      } catch (Throwable t) {\n        LOG.warn(\"Exception throws in the callback. Drop this event {}\", event, t);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n            .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_CALLBACK_EXCEPTION, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(), \"topic=\" + topic);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  start the executing thread and let the Sender run.\n   */\n  public synchronized void start() {\n    if (this.thread == null) {\n      thread = new Thread(this);\n      thread.setDaemon(true);\n      thread.setName(name);\n      thread.start();\n      LOG.warn(\n          \"[{}] created and started [{}] to let it dequeue LoggingAuditEvents and send to Kafka.\",\n          Thread.currentThread().getName(), name);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  reserve some time (by default 30 seconds at most)to let AuditEventKafkaSender to send out\n   *  LoggingAuditEvent in the queue and gracefully stop AuditEventKafkaSender.\n   */\n  public synchronized void stop() {\n    LOG.warn(\n        \"[{}] waits up to {} seconds to let [{}] send out LoggingAuditEvents left in the queue if\"\n            + \" any.\",\n        Thread.currentThread().getName(), stopGracePeriodInSeconds, name);\n    int i = 0;\n Thread.sleep(THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS * 1000);\n        CommonUtils.reportQueueUsage(queue.size(), queue.remainingCapacity(), host, stage.toString());\n        LOG.info(\"In {} round, [{}] waited {} seconds and the current queue size is {}\", i,\n            Thread.currentThread().getName(), THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS, queue.size());\n      } catch (InterruptedException e) {\n        LOG.warn(\"[{}] got interrupted while waiting for [{}] to send out LoggingAuditEvents left \"\n            + \"in the queue.\", Thread.currentThread().getName(), name, e);\n      }\n    }\n    cancelled.set(true);\n return;\n        }\n      }\n      currentPartitionId =  partitionInfoList.get(ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(\n          partitionInfoList.size())).partition();\n      LOG.warn(\"After {} trials, set current partition to {}\",\n          MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION, currentPartitionId);\n\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  Sender dequeues LoggingAuditEvents and sends them to Kafka cluster. If send to one partition\n   *  fails, it will choose another partition. For each event, it will try at most\n   *  NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING times (3 times) before dropping this event. Metrics are\n   *  used to track the queue size and usuage, number of events sent out to Kafka successfully, and\n   *  the number of events dropped.\n   */\n  @Override\n  public void run() {\n    LoggingAuditEvent event = null;\n    ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> record;\n    byte[] value = null;\n\n    while (!cancelled.get()) {\n      try {\n        refreshPartitionIfNeeded();\n        if of the deque.\n      // If number of tries reaches 3, meaning that 3 partitions have been tried sending to but\n      // still failed, this event is dropped.\n      Integer count = Drop this event.\");\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n        LOG.debug(\n            \"Enqueuing LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue was interrupted in callback. \"\n                + \"Drop this event\");\n        eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onCompletion(RecordMetadata recordMetadata, Exception e) {\n      try {\n        if (e == null) {\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_ACKED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n\n          // if send is successful, remove the event from the map eventTriedCount if it was added\n          // LoggingAuditHeaders can uniquely identify an event.\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          // if send out successfully, remove the partition from the badPartitions if it was added.\n          badPartitions.remove(recordMetadata.partition());\n        } else {\n          checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed();\n        }\n      } catch (Throwable t) {\n        LOG.warn(\"Exception throws in the callback. Drop this event {}\", event, t);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n            .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_CALLBACK_EXCEPTION, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(), \"topic=\" + topic);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  start the executing thread and let the Sender run.\n   */\n  public synchronized void start() {\n    if getClusterThreadPool() {\n    return clusterThreadPool;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Distribute the message batch into different partitions, and use the under-lying kafka writing\n   * thread pool to speed up writing.\n   *\n   * If skipNoLeaderPartitions flag is set, we will skip the partitions that have no leader.\n   *\n   * @param partitions unordered list of partitionInfo to be used for partitioning this batch\n   * @param topic the kafka topic\n   * @param logMessages the messages that will be written to kafka\n   * @param mapOfTrackedMessageMaps a map whose key is the partitionId, value is an inner map. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of tracked messages\n   * @param mapOfInvalidMessageMaps a map whose key is the partitionId, value is an inner map. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of invalid messages(note:\n   *                               invalid messages may or may not be tracked)\n   * @param mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket a map whose key is the partitionId, value is original\n   *                                       index within the bucket. The init value  is -1. Anytime\n   *                                       a message is assigned to a partition (may be skipped),\n   *                                       the value increases by 1. If certain messages are skipped\n   *                                       due to corruption, i.e. they are not added to the bucket,\n   *                                       the actual index of those un-skipped messages could be\n   *                                       lower. The original index means assuming no messages are\n   *                                       skipped.\n   *                                       For example: for ith partition, there are 6 message m0,\n   *                                       m1, m2, m3, m4, m5 assigned. The original index will be\n   *                                       0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5. If m1 and m3 are skipped due to\n   *                                       corruption, the actual messages sent to Kafka will be\n   *                                       m0, m2, m4, m5 and actual index will be 0, 1, 2, 3.\n   *\n   * @return a list of message lists that are classified based on partitions.\n   */\n  Map<Integer, List<ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]>>> messageCollation(\n      List<PartitionInfo> partitions,\n      String topic,\n      List<LogMessage> logMessages,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfTrackedMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfInvalidMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Integer> mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket) throws Exception {\n    LOG.info(\"Collate {} messages of topic {} for logStream {}\", logMessages.size(), topic, logName);\n\n    Map<Integer, List<ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]>>> buckets = new HashMap<>();\n    try {\n      List<PartitionInfo> validPartitions = partitions;\n      if (skipNoLeaderPartitions) {\n        validPartitions = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (PartitionInfo partitionInfo : partitions) {\n          // If there is no leader, the id value is -1\n          // github.com/apache/kafka/blob/trunk/clients/src/main/java/org/apache/kafka/common/PartitionInfo.java\n          if (partitionInfo.leader().id() >= 0) {\n            validPartitions.add(partitionInfo);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      for (int i = 0; i < validPartitions.size(); i++) {\n        // for each partitionId, there is a corresponding bucket in buckets and a corresponding\n        // trackedMessageMap in mapOfTrackedMessageMaps and corresponding invalidMessageMap in\n        // mapOfInvalidMessageMaps\n        int partitionId = validPartitions.get(i).partition();\n        buckets.put(partitionId, new ArrayList<>());\n        mapOfTrackedMessageMaps.put(partitionId, new HashMap<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>());\n        mapOfInvalidMessageMaps.put(partitionId, new HashMap<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>());\n        mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.put(partitionId, -1);\n      }\n\n      ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> keyedMessage;\n      for (LogMessage msg : logMessages) {\n        byte[] key = null;\n        if (msg.isSetKey()) {\n          key = msg.getKey();\n        }\n        int partitionId = partitioner.partition(key, validPartitions);\n        if (skipNoLeaderPartitions) {\n          partitionId = validPartitions.get(partitionId).partition();\n        }\n        keyedMessage = new ProducerRecord<>(topic, partitionId, key, msg.getMessage());\n        mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.put(partitionId, 1 + mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.get(partitionId));\n        Headers headers = keyedMessage.headers();\n        checkAndSetLoggingAuditHeadersForLogMessage(msg);\n        if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null) {\n          // check if the message should be skipped\n          if (checkMessageValidAndInjectHeaders(msg, headers, mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.get(partitionId),\n              partitionId, mapOfTrackedMessageMaps, mapOfInvalidMessageMaps)) {\n            continue;\n          }\n        }\n        buckets.get(partitionId).add(keyedMessage);\n      }\n    } catch (Exception e) {\n      LOG.error(\"Failed in message collation for topic {}, partitioner {}\", topic, partitioner.getClass().getName(), e);\n      throw new LogStreamWriterException(e.toString());\n    }\n    return buckets;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  Validate the message and inject headers for the ProducerRecord\n   *\n   *  If the message is corrupted and corrupted messages are configured to be skipped at the current\n   *  stage, return true. Otherwise, return false, since the message should not be skipped\n   *\n   * @param msg the message to validate\n   * @param headers the headers of the ProducerRecord\n   * @param indexWithinTheBucket the index of the current message within the bucket\n   * @param partitionId the partition id\n   * @param mapOfTrackedMessageMaps a map whose key is the partitionId, value is an inner map. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of tracked messages\n   * @param mapOfInvalidMessageMaps a map whose key is the partitionId, value is an inner map. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of invalid messages(note:\n   *                               invalid messages may or may not be tracked)\n   * @return a boolean indicates whether this message should be skipped\n   */\n  public boolean checkMessageValidAndInjectHeaders(\n      LogMessage msg, Headers headers, int indexWithinTheBucket, int partitionId,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfTrackedMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfInvalidMessageMaps) {\n    boolean isMessageValid = checkMessageValid(msg);\n\n    // note that only a percentage of messages are tracked, i.e. corresponding audit events are sent\n    // out at different stages. For each bucket, messages being tracked are kept in the hash map where\n    // key is the indexWithin the bucket, and value is the message LoggingAuditHeaders.\n    if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null && msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().isTracked()){\n      mapOfTrackedMessageMaps.get(partitionId).put(indexWithinTheBucket, msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n    }\n\n    // note that some messages (whether being tracked or not) could be invalid because crc32 checksum\n    // does not match mismatch or original message cannot be deserialized. These invalid messages are\n    // kept in the hash map where key is the indexWithin the bucket, and value is the message\n    // LoggingAuditHeaders.\n    if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null && !isMessageValid) {\n      mapOfInvalidMessageMaps.get(partitionId).put(indexWithinTheBucket, msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n    }\n\n    boolean shouldSkipMessage = false;\n    if (enableLoggingAudit && auditConfig.isSkipCorruptedMessageAtCurrentStage() && !isMessageValid) {\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.AUDIT_NUM_INVALID_MESSAGES_SKIPPED, \"topic=\" + topic,\n          \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logName=\" + msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName(),\n          \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      shouldSkipMessage = true;\n    }\n    if (this.headersInjector != null) {\n      injectHeadersForProducerRecord(msg, headers);\n    }\n    return shouldSkipMessage;\n  }\n\n  protected void injectHeadersForProducerRecord(LogMessage msg, Headers headers) {\n    try {\n      if (msg.isSetLoggingAuditHeaders()) {\n        byte[] serializedAuditHeaders = SERIALIZER.get().serialize(msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n        this.headersInjector.addHeaders(headers, LOGGING_AUDIT_HEADER_KEY, serializedAuditHeaders);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.AUDIT_HEADERS_INJECTED,  \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      }\n      if (msg.isSetChecksum()) {\n        this.headersInjector.addHeaders(headers, CRC_HEADER_KEY, Longs.toByteArray(msg.getChecksum()));\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.CHECKSUM_INJECTED,  \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      }\n    } catch (TException e) {\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.NUMBER_OF_SERIALIZING_HEADERS_ERRORS);\n      LOG.warn(\"Exception thrown while serializing headers\", e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  protected boolean checkMessageValid(LogMessage msg) {\n    if (msg.getMessage() == null){\n      return false;\n    }\n    boolean isMessageUncorrupted = true;\n    boolean canDeserializeMessage = true;\n\n    // check if message is corrupted based on crc32 checksum\n    if (msg.isSetChecksum()) {\n      long start = System.nanoTime();\n      long singerChecksum = computeCRC(msg.getMessage());\n      isMessageUncorrupted = singerChecksum == msg.getChecksum();\n The original index means assuming no messages are\n   *                                       skipped.\n   *                                       For example: for ith partition, there are 6 message m0,\n   *                                       m1, m2, m3, m4, m5 assigned. The original index will be\n   *                                       0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5. index will be 0, 1, 2, 3.\n   *\n   * @return a list of message lists that are classified based on partitions.\n   */\n  Map<Integer, List<ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]>>> messageCollation(\n      List<PartitionInfo> partitions,\n      String topic,\n      List<LogMessage> logMessages,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfTrackedMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfInvalidMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Integer> mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket) throws Exception {\n    LOG.info(\"Collate {} messages of topic {} for logStream {}\", logMessages.size(), topic, logName);\n\n    Map<Integer, List<ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]>>> buckets = new HashMap<>();\n    try {\n      List<PartitionInfo> validPartitions = partitions;\n      if (skipNoLeaderPartitions) {\n        validPartitions = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (PartitionInfo partitionInfo : partitions) {\n          // If there is no leader, the id value is -1\n          // github.com/apache/kafka/blob/trunk/clients/src/main/java/org/apache/kafka/common/PartitionInfo.java\n          if (partitionInfo.leader().id() >= 0) {\n            validPartitions.add(partitionInfo);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      for (int i = 0; i < validPartitions.size(); i++) {\n        // for each partitionId, there is a corresponding bucket in buckets and a corresponding\n        // trackedMessageMap in mapOfTrackedMessageMaps and corresponding invalidMessageMap in\n        // mapOfInvalidMessageMaps\n        int partitionId = validPartitions.get(i).partition();\n        buckets.put(partitionId, new ArrayList<>());\n        mapOfTrackedMessageMaps.put(partitionId, new HashMap<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>());\n        mapOfInvalidMessageMaps.put(partitionId, new HashMap<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>());\n        mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.put(partitionId, -1);\n      }\n\n      ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> keyedMessage;\n      for (LogMessage msg : logMessages) {\n        byte[] key = null;\n        if (msg.isSetKey()) {\n          key = msg.getKey();\n        }\n        int partitionId = partitioner.partition(key, validPartitions);\n        if (skipNoLeaderPartitions) {\n          partitionId = validPartitions.get(partitionId).partition();\n        }\n        keyedMessage = new ProducerRecord<>(topic, partitionId, key, msg.getMessage());\n        mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.put(partitionId, 1 + mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.get(partitionId));\n        Headers headers = continue;\n          }\n        }\n        buckets.get(partitionId).add(keyedMessage);\n      }\n    } catch (Exception e) {\n      LOG.error(\"Failed in message collation for topic {}, partitioner {}\", topic, partitioner.getClass().getName(), e);\n      throw new LogStreamWriterException(e.toString());\n    }\n    return buckets;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  Validate the message and inject headers for the ProducerRecord\n   *\n   *  If the message is corrupted and corrupted messages are configured to be skipped at the current\n   *  stage, return true. For each bucket, messages being tracked are kept in the hash map where\n    // key is the indexWithin the bucket, and value is the message LoggingAuditHeaders.\n    if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null && msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().isTracked()){\n      mapOfTrackedMessageMaps.get(partitionId).put(indexWithinTheBucket, msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n    }\n\n    // note that some messages (whether being tracked or not) could be invalid because crc32 checksum\n    // does not match mismatch or original message cannot be deserialized. These invalid messages are\n    // kept in the hash map where key is the indexWithin the bucket, and value is the message\n    // LoggingAuditHeaders.\n    if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null && !isMessageValid) {\n      mapOfInvalidMessageMaps.get(partitionId).put(indexWithinTheBucket, msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n    }\n\n    boolean shouldSkipMessage = false;\n    if (enableLoggingAudit && auditConfig.isSkipCorruptedMessageAtCurrentStage() && !isMessageValid) {\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.AUDIT_NUM_INVALID_MESSAGES_SKIPPED, \"topic=\" + topic,\n          \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logName=\" + msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName(),\n          \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      shouldSkipMessage = true;\n    }\n    if (this.headersInjector != null) {\n      injectHeadersForProducerRecord(msg, headers);\n    }\n    return shouldSkipMessage;\n  }\n\n  protected void injectHeadersForProducerRecord(LogMessage msg, Headers headers) {\n    try {\n \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      }\n    } catch (TException e) {\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.NUMBER_OF_SERIALIZING_HEADERS_ERRORS);\n      LOG.warn(\"Exception thrown while serializing headers\", e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  protected boolean checkMessageValid(LogMessage msg) {\n    if (msg.getMessage() == null){\n      return false;\n    }\n    boolean isMessageUncorrupted = true;\n    boolean canDeserializeMessage = true;\n\n    // check if message is corrupted based on crc32 checksum\n    if (msg.isSetChecksum()) {\n      long start = System.nanoTime();\n      long singerChecksum = computeCRC(msg.getMessage());\n      isMessageUncorrupted = singerChecksum == msg.getChecksum();\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.gauge(SingerMetrics.AUDIT_COMPUTE_CHECKSUM_LATENCY_NANO, Math.max(\n          0, System.nanoTime() - start), \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName(), \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n        if (loggingAuditHeaders.isTracked()) {\n java.io.UnsupportedEncodingException;\nimport java.nio.ByteBuffer;\n\n/**\n * A singleton class to serialize and deserialize a thrift object.\n * This class is thread safe.\n *\n * Thrift supports both the TBinaryProtocol and the TCompactProtocol.\n * From benchmarking, TCompactProtocol is between 20% to 30% more\n * efficient in space usage for large thrift objects. We originally\n * encoded most of our data in TBinaryProtocol. To support both,\n * we now can deserialize data encoded in TBinaryProtocol or in a special\n * \"prefixed\" TCompactProtocol with the deserialize() call. We\n * can choose to serialize data using TBinaryProtocol (the default), or\n * using the \"prefixed\" TCompactProtocol (serializePrefixed call).\n *\n * TBinaryProtocol's first byte is always field type (see TType.java in\n * thrift), which ranges from 0-16. So by prefixing the TCompactProtocol\n * output with SECRET_BYTE (0xff) we can distinguish between TBinaryProtocol\n * and TCompactProtocol and know which one to use.\n *\n * Usage:\n *  // Serialize using TBinaryProtocol.\n *  ThriftCodec.getInstance().serialize(myThriftObject)\n *  // Serialize using prefixed TCompactProtocol.\n *  ThriftCodec.getInstance().serializePrefixed(myThriftObject)\n *  // Deserialize any of the above two protocols:\n *  ThriftObject myThriftObject = ThriftCodec.deserialize(myBytes, ThriftObject.class);\n *\n */\npublic class ThriftCodec {\n  // CHECKSTYLE_OFF: DeclarationOrder\n\n  private static volatile ThriftCodec instance;\n\n  private ThriftCodec() {\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * get an instance of the singleton object.\n   */\n  public static ThriftCodec getInstance() {\n    if (instance == null) {\n      synchronized (ThriftCodec.class) {\n        if (instance == null) {\n          instance = new ThriftCodec();\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return instance;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Deserialize a thrift value encoded using TCompactProtocol.\n   */\n  public static <T extends TBase> T deserializeCompact(byte[] value, Class<T> thriftClass)\n      throws TException {\n    ThriftCodec codec = getInstance();\n    try {\n      T obj = thriftClass.newInstance();\n      codec.deserializeCompact(obj, value);\n      return obj;\n    } catch (InstantiationException e) {\n      throw new RuntimeException(e);\n    } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {\n      throw new RuntimeException(e);\n    }\n  }\n\n\n  /**\n   * Serialize a thrift object.\n   *\n   * @param obj A thrift object.\n   */\n  public <T extends TBase> byte[] serialize(T obj) throws TException {\n    byte[] bytes = encoder.get().serialize(obj);\n    return bytes;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Serializes thrift objects with the TCompactProtocol\n   * prefixed with SECRET_BYTE so that we can differentiate\n   * between these and TBinaryProtocol datasets.\n   *\n   * @param obj a thrift object.\n   */\n  public <T extends TBase> byte[] serializePrefixed(T obj) throws TException {\n    byte[] bytes = prefixedEncoder.get().serialize(obj);\n    return bytes;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Serialize a thrift object as JSON.\n   *\n   * @param obj A thrift object.\n   */\n  public static <T extends TBase> String serializeJson(T obj) throws TException {\n    ByteArrayOutputStream byteArrayOutputStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n    obj.write(new TJSONProtocol(new TIOStreamTransport(byteArrayOutputStream)));\n    try {\n      return byteArrayOutputStream.toString(\"UTF-8\");\n    } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException e) {\n      throw new TException(e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Deserialize a thrift object encoded using the TJSONProtocol\n   */\n  public static <T extends TBase> T deserializeJson(String jsonValue, Class<T> thriftClass)\n      throws TException {\n    ByteArrayInputStream byteArrayInputStream =\n writing.\n   *\n   * If skipNoLeaderPartitions flag is set, we will skip the partitions that have no leader.\n   *\n   * @param partitions unordered list of partitionInfo to be used for partitioning this batch\n   * @param topic the kafka topic\n   * @param logMessages the messages that will be written to kafka\n   * @param mapOfTrackedMessageMaps a map whose key is the partitionId, value is an inner map. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of tracked messages\n   * @param mapOfInvalidMessageMaps a map whose key is the partitionId, value is an inner map. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of invalid messages(note:\n   *                               invalid messages may or may not be tracked)\n   * @param mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket a map whose key is the partitionId, value is original\n   *                                       index within the bucket. The init value  is -1. Anytime\n   *                                       a message is assigned to a partition (may be skipped),\n   *                                       the value increases by 1. If certain messages are skipped\n   *                                       due to corruption, i.e. they are not added to the bucket,\n   *                                       the actual index of those un-skipped messages could be\n   *                                       lower. The original index means assuming no messages are\n   *                                       skipped.\n   *                                       For example: for ith partition, there are 6 message m0,\n   *                                       m1, m2, m3, m4, m5 assigned. The original index will be\n   *                                       0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5. If m1 and m3 are skipped due to\n   *                                       corruption, the actual messages sent to Kafka will be\n   *                                       m0, m2, m4, m5 and actual index will be 0, 1, 2, 3.\n   *\n   * @return a list of message lists that are classified based on partitions.\n   */\n  Map<Integer, List<ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]>>> messageCollation(\n      List<PartitionInfo> partitions,\n      String topic,\n      List<LogMessage> logMessages,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfTrackedMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfInvalidMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Integer> mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket) throws Exception {\n    LOG.info(\"Collate {} messages of topic {} for logStream {}\", logMessages.size(), topic, logName);\n\n    Map<Integer, List<ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]>>> buckets = new HashMap<>();\n    try {\n      List<PartitionInfo> validPartitions = partitions;\n      if (skipNoLeaderPartitions) {\n        validPartitions = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (PartitionInfo partitionInfo : partitions) {\n          // If there is no leader, the id value is -1\n          // github.com/apache/kafka/blob/trunk/clients/src/main/java/org/apache/kafka/common/PartitionInfo.java\n          if (partitionInfo.leader().id() >= 0) {\n            validPartitions.add(partitionInfo);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      for (int i = 0; i < validPartitions.size(); i++) {\n        // for each partitionId, there is a corresponding bucket in buckets and a corresponding\n        // trackedMessageMap in mapOfTrackedMessageMaps and corresponding invalidMessageMap in\n        // mapOfInvalidMessageMaps\n        int partitionId = validPartitions.get(i).partition();\n        buckets.put(partitionId, new ArrayList<>());\n        mapOfTrackedMessageMaps.put(partitionId, new HashMap<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>());\n        mapOfInvalidMessageMaps.put(partitionId, new HashMap<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>());\n        mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.put(partitionId, -1);\n      }\n\n      ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> keyedMessage;\n      for (LogMessage msg : logMessages) {\n        byte[] key = null;\n        if (msg.isSetKey()) {\n          key = msg.getKey();\n        }\n        int partitionId = partitioner.partition(key, validPartitions);\n        if (skipNoLeaderPartitions) {\n          partitionId = validPartitions.get(partitionId).partition();\n        }\n        keyedMessage = new ProducerRecord<>(topic, partitionId, key, msg.getMessage());\n        mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.put(partitionId, 1 + mapOfOriginalIndexWithinBucket.get(partitionId));\n        Headers headers = keyedMessage.headers();\n        checkAndSetLoggingAuditHeadersForLogMessage(msg);\n        if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null) {\n          // check if the message should be skipped\n continue;\n          }\n        }\n        buckets.get(partitionId).add(keyedMessage);\n      }\n    } catch (Exception e) {\n      LOG.error(\"Failed in message collation for topic {}, partitioner {}\", topic, partitioner.getClass().getName(), e);\n      throw new LogStreamWriterException(e.toString());\n    }\n    return buckets;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  Validate the message and inject headers for the ProducerRecord\n   *\n   *  If the message is corrupted and corrupted messages are configured to be skipped at the current\n   *  stage, return true. The\n   *                               key of the inner map is indexWithinTheBucket and the value of the\n   *                               inner map is LoggingAuditHeaders object of invalid messages(note:\n   *                               invalid messages may or may not be tracked)\n   * @return a boolean indicates whether this message should be skipped\n   */\n  public boolean checkMessageValidAndInjectHeaders(\n      LogMessage msg, Headers headers, int indexWithinTheBucket, int partitionId,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfTrackedMessageMaps,\n      Map<Integer, Map<Integer, LoggingAuditHeaders>> mapOfInvalidMessageMaps) {\n    boolean isMessageValid = checkMessageValid(msg);\n\n    // note that only a percentage of messages are tracked, i.e. corresponding audit events are sent\n    // out at different stages. For each bucket, messages being tracked are kept in the hash map where\n    // key is the indexWithin the bucket, and value is the message LoggingAuditHeaders.\n    if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null && msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().isTracked()){\n      mapOfTrackedMessageMaps.get(partitionId).put(indexWithinTheBucket, msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n    }\n\n    // note that some messages (whether being tracked or not) could be invalid because crc32 checksum\n    // does not match mismatch or original message cannot be deserialized. These invalid messages are\n    // kept in the hash map where key is the indexWithin the bucket, and value is the message\n    // LoggingAuditHeaders.\n    if (msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders() != null && !isMessageValid) {\n      mapOfInvalidMessageMaps.get(partitionId).put(indexWithinTheBucket, msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n    }\n\n    boolean shouldSkipMessage = false;\n    if (enableLoggingAudit && auditConfig.isSkipCorruptedMessageAtCurrentStage() && !isMessageValid) {\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.AUDIT_NUM_INVALID_MESSAGES_SKIPPED, \"topic=\" + topic,\n          \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logName=\" + msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName(),\n          \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      shouldSkipMessage = true;\n    }\n    if (this.headersInjector != null) {\n      injectHeadersForProducerRecord(msg, headers);\n    }\n    return shouldSkipMessage;\n  }\n\n  protected void injectHeadersForProducerRecord(LogMessage msg, Headers headers) {\n    try {\n \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n      }\n    } catch (TException e) {\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(SingerMetrics.NUMBER_OF_SERIALIZING_HEADERS_ERRORS);\n      LOG.warn(\"Exception thrown while serializing headers\", e);\n    }\n  }\n\n  protected boolean checkMessageValid(LogMessage msg) {\n    if (msg.getMessage() == null){\n      return false;\n    }\n OpenTsdbMetricConverter.gauge(SingerMetrics.AUDIT_COMPUTE_CHECKSUM_LATENCY_NANO, Math.max(\n          0, System.nanoTime() - start), \"host=\" + HOSTNAME, \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n msg.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName(), \"logStreamName=\" + logName);\n        if (loggingAuditHeaders.isTracked()) {\n Note that, this is different from the retry handled by\n   *  kafka client\n   *  library when sending an event to a certain partition.\n   */\n  private static final int NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING = 3;\n\n  /**\n   *  maximum time to wait when sender tries to dequeue events before returning null.\n   */\n  private static final int DEQUEUE_WAIT_IN_SECONDS = 30;\n\n\n  /**\n   *   when gracefully shutting down the Sender, the calling thread sleeps for some time and let\n   *   the sender to send out audit events left in the queue if there are any.\n   *   stopGracePeriodInSeconds is the maximum time reserved and\n   *   stopGracePeriodInSeconds / THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS is the total rounds the calling thread\n   *   will sleep.\n   */\n  private static final int THREAD_SLEEP_IN_SECONDS = 10;\n\n  /**\n   *  when gracefully shutting down the Sender, this field specifies maximum time for main thread\n   *  to wait, in order to let the sender send out audit events left in the queue if there are any.\n   */\n  private int stopGracePeriodInSeconds = 300;\n\n\n  /**\n   * Logging audit stage, can be THRIFTLOGGER, SINGER, MERCED and so on\n   */\n  private final LoggingAuditStage stage;\n\n  /**\n   * host name\n   */\n  private final String host;\n\n  /**\n   *  LinkedBlockingDequeue to store the LoggingAuditEvents.  The max capacity is specified when\n   *  creating this deque in the LoggingAuditClient.java\n   *\n   *  The AuditEventKafkaSender dequeue from the beginning of the deque, if does not send out event\n   *  successfully, it will enqueue this event to the beginning of the queue.  Note that, if enqueue\n   *  the failed event at the end of the queue, this event could be processed with quite some delay\n   *  and this is not the behavior we want.  That's we use LinkedBlockingDequeue,\n   *  not ArrayBlockingQueue.\n   */\n  private final LinkedBlockingDeque<LoggingAuditEvent> queue;\n\n  /**\n   * KafkaProducer instance to send audit events\n   */\n  private KafkaProducer<byte[], byte[]> kafkaProducer;\n\n  /**\n   * Serialize key and value to byte[]\n   */\n  private TSerializer serializer;\n\n  /**\n   *  flag to control the start and stop of the executing thread.\n   */\n  private AtomicBoolean cancelled = new AtomicBoolean(false);\n\n  /**\n   *  topic to store the audit events\n   */\n  private String topic;\n\n  /**\n   *  name of this sender instance\n   */\n  private String name;\n\n  /**\n   *  executing thread\n   */\n  private Thread thread;\n\n  /**\n   *  List of PartitionInfo\n   */\n  private List<PartitionInfo> partitionInfoList = new ArrayList<>();\n\n  /**\n   *  last time when partition list was refreshed. we want to refresh partition list every 5 mins.\n   */\n  private long lastTimeUpdate = -1;\n\n  /**\n   *  If sending out to one partition fails, this partition is added to set;\n   *  If sending out to one partition succeeds, this partition is removed if it was added before.\n   */\n  private Set<Integer> badPartitions = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();\n\n  /**\n   * For each event (identified by LoggingAuditHeaders, key of the map), track the number of tries\n   * for sending to Kafka. Each try will choose a different and partition that is not in the\n   * badPartitions.  When event is send out successfully or dropped, the corresponding entry in\n   * this map is removed.\n   */\n\n  private Map<LoggingAuditHeaders, Integer> eventTriedCount = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();\n\n  /**\n   *  currentPartitionId specifies the partition of audit_event topic used to receive audit events.\n   *  The currentPartitionId will be reset in resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded() method. This reduces\n   *  the number of TCP connections from audit client to the Kafka Cluster hosting the audit_event\n   *  topic.\n   */\n  private int currentPartitionId = -1;\n\n  public AuditEventKafkaSender(KafkaSenderConfig config,\n                               LinkedBlockingDeque<LoggingAuditEvent> queue,\n                               LoggingAuditStage stage, String host, config.getStopGracePeriodInSeconds();\n    this.badPartitions.add(-1);\n    try {\n      this.serializer = new TSerializer();\n    } catch (TTransportException e) {\n      throw new RuntimeException(\"Failed to create TSerializer\", e);\n    }\n  }\n\n\n  public KafkaProducer<byte[], byte[]> getKafkaProducer() {\n    return kafkaProducer;\n  }\n\n  public void setKafkaProducer(KafkaProducer<byte[], byte[]> kafkaProducer) {\n    this.kafkaProducer = kafkaProducer;\n  }\n\n  private void refreshPartitionIfNeeded() {\n    // refresh every 30 seconds\n    if (System.currentTimeMillis() - lastTimeUpdate > 1000 * PARTITIONS_REFRESH_INTERVAL_IN_SECONDS) {\n      try {\n        badPartitions.clear();\n        badPartitions.add(-1);\n        partitionInfoList = this.kafkaProducer.partitionsFor(topic);\n        lastTimeUpdate = System.currentTimeMillis();\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n            LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITIONS_REFRESH_COUNT, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n      } catch (Exception e) {\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n            LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITIONS_REFRESH_ERROR, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n      }\n    }\n    resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded();\n  }\n\n  private void resetCurrentPartitionIdIfNeeded() {\n    if (partitionInfoList.size() == 0) {\n      currentPartitionId = -1;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (badPartitions.contains(currentPartitionId)){\n      int trial = 0;\n      while (trial < MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION) {\n        trial += 1;\n        int index = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(partitionInfoList.size());\n        int randomPartition = partitionInfoList.get(index).partition();\n        if (!badPartitions.contains(randomPartition)) {\n          LOG.warn(\"Change current partition of audit event topic from {} to {}\", currentPartitionId,\n              randomPartition);\n          currentPartitionId = randomPartition;\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter.incr(\n              LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_CURRENT_PARTITION_RESET, 1,\n              \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString());\n          return;\n        }\n      }\n      currentPartitionId =  partitionInfoList.get(ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(\n          partitionInfoList.size())).partition();\n      LOG.warn(\"After {} trials, set current partition to {}\",\n          MAX_RETRIES_FOR_SELECTION_RANDOM_PARTITION, currentPartitionId);\n\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  Sender dequeues LoggingAuditEvents and sends them to Kafka cluster. If send to one partition\n   *  fails, it will choose another partition. For each event, it will try at most\n   *  NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING times (3 times) before dropping this event. Metrics are\n   *  used to track the queue size and usuage, number of events sent out to Kafka successfully, and\n   *  the number of events dropped.\n   */\n  @Override\n  public void run() {\n    LoggingAuditEvent event = null;\n    ProducerRecord<byte[], byte[]> record;\n    byte[] value = null;\n\n    while (!cancelled.get()) {\n      try {\n        refreshPartitionIfNeeded();\n        if (currentPartitionId == -1){\n          Thread.sleep(100);\n          continue;\n        }\n        event = queue.poll(DEQUEUE_WAIT_IN_SECONDS, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n        if (event != null) {\n }\n  }\n\n\n  public class KafkaProducerCallback implements Callback {\n\n    private LoggingAuditEvent event;\n    private int partition;\n\n    public KafkaProducerCallback(LoggingAuditEvent event, partition is added to badPartitions.\n      badPartitions.add(this.partition);\n      OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n          .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_PARTITION_ERROR, 1,\n              \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(), \"topic=\" + topic,\n              \"partition=\" + this.partition);\n\n      // retry the failed event by inserting it at the beginning of the deque.\n      // If number of tries reaches 3, meaning that 3 partitions have been tried sending to but\n      // still failed, this event is dropped.\n      Integer count = OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n            .gauge(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_RETRIED,\n                eventTriedCount.size(), \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                \"topic=\" + topic);\n      } else if (count >= NUM_OF_PARTITIONS_TO_TRY_SENDING) {\n          LOG.debug(\"Failed to send audit event after trying {} partitions. Drop event.\", count);\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_DROPPED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      } else {\n          eventTriedCount.put(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders(), count + 1);\n          insertEvent(event);\n      }\n    }\n\n    public void insertEvent(LoggingAuditEvent event){\n      try {\n        boolean success = queue.offerFirst(event, 3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);\n        if (!success) {\n          LOG.debug(\"Failed to enqueue LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue when executing \"\n              + \"producer send callback. Drop this event.\");\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n        }\n      } catch (InterruptedException ex) {\n        LOG.debug(\n            \"Enqueuing LoggingAuditEvent at head of the queue was interrupted in callback. \"\n                + \"Drop this event\");\n        eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n      }\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onCompletion(RecordMetadata recordMetadata, Exception e) {\n      try {\n        if (e == null) {\n          OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n              .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_EVENTS_ACKED, 1,\n                  \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(),\n                  \"logName=\" + event.getLoggingAuditHeaders().getLogName());\n\n          // if send is successful, remove the event from the map eventTriedCount if it was added\n          // LoggingAuditHeaders can uniquely identify an event.\n          eventTriedCount.remove(event.getLoggingAuditHeaders());\n          // if send out successfully, remove the partition from the badPartitions if it was added.\n          badPartitions.remove(recordMetadata.partition());\n        } else {\n          checkAndEnqueueWhenSendFailed();\n        }\n      } catch (Throwable t) {\n        LOG.warn(\"Exception throws in the callback. Drop this event {}\", event, t);\n        OpenTsdbMetricConverter\n            .incr(LoggingAuditClientMetrics.AUDIT_CLIENT_SENDER_KAFKA_CALLBACK_EXCEPTION, 1,\n                \"host=\" + host, \"stage=\" + stage.toString(), \"topic=\" + topic);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  start the executing thread and let the Sender run.\n   */\n  public synchronized void start() {\n    if (this.thread == null) {\n      thread = new Thread(this);\n      thread.setDaemon(true);\n      thread.setName(name);\n      thread.start();\n      LOG.warn(\n          \"[{}] created and started [{}] to let it dequeue LoggingAuditEvents and send to Kafka.\",\n          Thread.currentThread().getName(), name);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   *  reserve some time (by default 30 seconds at most)to let AuditEventKafkaSender to send out\n   *  LoggingAuditEvent in the queue and gracefully stop AuditEventKafkaSender.\n   */\n  public synchronized void stop() {\n    LOG.warn(\n        \"[{}] waits up
